==========================================================================================
S(lam1 lam2) = sum_{sg1,sg2 in {L,+,-}}  beta_{sg1} beta_{sg2} I^{lam1 lam2}_{sg1,sg2;f}
Each soft longitudinal (L) line carries one beta factor;
transverse (+/-) lines carry none (beta_L = beta, beta_+/- = 1).
beta is kept symbolic (no nu dependence).  Sum done at the
J-seed level (Scheme A).  NO sin^2+cos^2=1 reduction applied.
S  =  sum_{(r,m,n)} C_{rmn} * J_r(m,n)
==========================================================================================
